<p class="sk-key"><span class="k-up">green</span> = rising (f′ &gt; 0), <span class="k-down">red</span> = falling (f′ &lt; 0), <span class="k-ext">●</span> max/min, <span class="k-ip">○</span> inflection point (f″ changes sign), <span class="k-asym">dashed</span> = asymptote. The strips under the graph are your sign maps: ∪ concave up, ∩ concave down.</p>

          0</pre><p class="note">‖ = not in the domain (vertical asymptote). <p class="sk-key"><span class="k-up">green</span> = rising (f′ &gt; 0), <span class="k-down">red</span> = falling (f′ &lt; 0), <span class="k-ext">●</span> max/min, <span class="k-ip">○</span> inflection point (f″ changes sign), <span class="k-asym">dashed</span> = asymptote. The strips under the graph are your sign maps: ∪ concave up, ∩ concave down.</p>

<p class="sk-key"><span class="k-up">green</span> = rising (f′ &gt; 0), <span class="k-down">red</span> = falling (f′ &lt; 0), <span class="k-ext">●</span> max/min, <span class="k-ip">○</span> inflection point (f″ changes sign), <span class="k-asym">dashed</span> = asymptote. The strips under the graph are your sign maps: ∪ concave up, ∩ concave down.</p>
